I feel like I could easily handle it all if my brain would just stop jumping from one thing to the next.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced periods in the past where you felt the exact opposite—such as feeling very down, sad, or losing interest in almost everything for at least two weeks?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really experienced periods of feeling very down, sad, or losing interest like that. My main struggles have mostly just been some anxiety in the past, and then this recent high-energy distractibility where my mind is constantly racing.\n\nDoctor: Is this the very first time in your life you've experienced a period of high energy and racing thoughts like this, or have you had similar phases in the past?\n\nPatient: I'm not quite sure, but I don't think I've ever experienced phases this intense in the past. I've had some anxiety before, but these specific feelings of high energy and racing thoughts really only became a major issue over the last seven months since I switched jobs.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned having a hard time keeping up with your job. Has this high energy or distractibility caused severe disruptions, like major trouble at work or serious conflicts with people close to you?\n\nPatient: Yes, it has caused major disruptions at both work and home. I nearly lost a key account because of my poor follow-through, and my boss has warned me about my erratic hours and overpromising. On top of that, my wife is furious and worried because I'm missing family events and running up credit card debt on gear for projects I never finish.\n\nDoctor: Even if they weren't as intense as what you're experiencing now, have you ever had any past periods lasting at least a few days where you felt unusually energized, active, or needed much less sleep than normal?"
  }
]
========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
No, I haven't really experienced periods like that before my job transition seven months ago. These intense feelings of high energy, needing less sleep, and constantly being on the go only started becoming noticeable after I made the switch to the regional sales role.
